statements involve substantial risks and uncertainties. All statements, other than statements of historical facts, included in this report
regarding our strategy, future operations, future financial position, future revenues, projected costs, prospects, plans and objectives
of management are forward-looking statements. These statements are subject to risks and uncertainties and are based on information currently
available to our management. Words such as &#8220;anticipate,&#8221; &#8220;believe,&#8221; &#8220;estimate,&#8221; &#8220;expect,&#8221;
&#8220;intend,&#8221; &#8220;may,&#8221; &#8220;plan,&#8221; &#8220;contemplates,&#8221; &#8220;predict,&#8221; &#8220;project,&#8221;

forward-looking statements. The events and circumstances reflected in our forward-looking statements may not occur and actual results

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><b>APPTECH PAYMENTS CORP. </b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span id="q1_010"></span><b>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">(in thousands, except per share data)</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center">&#160;</p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000425" name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_80C_eus-gaap--OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock_zxNciCHM1Wk2" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>NOTE 1 &#8211; <span id="xdx_824_z8r9CxphJ2Tg">ORGANIZATION AND
DESCRIPTION OF BUSINESS</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">AppTech Payments Corp. (&#8220;AppTech&#8221;
or the &#8220;Company&#8221;), a Delaware corporation, is a Fintech Company headquartered in Carlsbad, California. AppTech utilizes innovative
payment processing and digital banking technologies to complement its core merchant services capabilities. The Company&#8217;s proprietary
software will provide progressive and adaptable products that are available through a suite of synergistic offerings directly to merchants,
banking institutions, and business enterprises.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">AppTech has a highly secure digital payments platform
that we acquired and are further developing digital banking products to power commerce experiences for clients and their customers. Based
upon industry standards for payment and banking protocols, we will offer standalone products and fully integrated solutions that deliver
innovative, unparalleled payments, banking, and financial services experiences. Our processing technologies can be taken off-the-shelf
or tapped into via our RESTful APIs to build fully branded and customizable experiences while supporting tokenized, multi-channel, and
multi-method transactions.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">AppTech trades under the symbol &#8220;APCX&#8221;
and its warrants trade under the symbol &#8220;APCXW&#8221;.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Purchase of Infinitus Pay, Inc.</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On October 31, 2025 (the &#8220;Acquisition Date&#8221;),
the Company acquired 100% of the outstanding equity interests of Infinitus Pay Inc. (&#8220;IP&#8221;), a provider of payment platform
solutions, pursuant to a Stock Purchase and Share Exchange Agreement dated October 30, 2025. The acquisition was accounted for as a business
combination in accordance with ASC 805, <i>Business Combinations</i>. The acquisition enhances the Company&#8217;s Banking-as-a-Service
(BaaS) and digital commerce offerings by integrating IP&#8217;s technology platform and customer relationships.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Total consideration transferred was $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferred1_pn3n3_c20251030__20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfinitusPayIncMember_zAgAGrWDblJe" title="Acquisition, consideration transferred"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferred1" contextRef="From2025-10-302025-10-31_custom_InfinitusPayIncMember" id="Fact000427" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">3,555</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand,
consisting of $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesGross_pn3n3_c20251030__20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfinitusPayIncMember_zetwCccBlTMk" title="Business acquisition, cash paid"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:PaymentsToAcquireBusinessesGross" contextRef="From2025-10-302025-10-31_custom_InfinitusPayIncMember" id="Fact000429" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">2,000</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in cash, $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferredEquityInterestsIssuedAndIssuable_pn3n3_c20251030__20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfinitusPayIncMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__us-gaap--CommonStockMember_zs1BKiyjg795" title="Business acquisition, common stock issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferredEquityInterestsIssuedAndIssuable" contextRef="From2025-10-302025-10-31_custom_InfinitusPayIncMember_us-gaap_CommonStockMember" id="Fact000431" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">800</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in common stock (valued at the closing market price on the acquisition date), $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferredEquityInterestsIssuedAndIssuable_pn3n3_c20251030__20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfinitusPayIncMember__us-gaap--StatementClassOfStockAxis__custom--WarrantsMember_zuS80Qr7cI5e" title="Business acquisition, common stock issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationConsiderationTransferredEquityInterestsIssuedAndIssuable" contextRef="From2025-10-302025-10-31_custom_InfinitusPayIncMember_custom_WarrantsMember" id="Fact000433" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">365</ix:nonFraction></span>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The purchase price was allocated to the
identifiable assets acquired and liabilities assumed based on estimated fair values at the acquisition date. Identifiable intangible
assets totaled $<span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedNet_iI_pn3n3_c20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfinitusPayIncMember_zIm3GTVQjgHc" title="Business acquistion, net identifiable assets acquired"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedNet" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-31_custom_InfinitusPayIncMember" id="Fact000437" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,760</ix:nonFraction></span>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Liquidity and Going Concern Considerations</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company has experienced recurring operating
losses, primarily due to limited revenues and net cash used in operations. The Company's current financial conditions and recurring losses
raise substantial doubt about its ability to continue as a going concern.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.5in">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In late 2024, we reorganized senior leadership
and initiated actions to reduce indebtedness to improve the current financial condition. In addition, we are actively pursuing additional
funding options and are confident that our revenue streams will begin generating cash, although no assurances can be made.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.5in">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Management continues to maintain adequate working
capital and adhere to prudent financial forecasting.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000449" name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock"><p id="xdx_800_eus-gaap--SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock_zpGGfbVqYZ45" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>NOTE 2 &#8211; <span id="xdx_825_z5YrizwIUPie">SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
POLICIES</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000451" name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_842_eus-gaap--BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zdHEk8vaJn59" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_862_zYqabz5tUfL4">Basis of Presentation</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The accompanying unaudited condensed consolidated
financial statements have been prepared in accordance with accounting principles generally accepted in the United States of America (&#8220;U.S.
GAAP&#8221;) for interim financial information and the rules and regulations of the Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;).
In the opinion of the Company&#8217;s management, the accompanying financial statements reflect all adjustments, consisting of normal,
recurring adjustments, considered necessary for a fair presentation of the results for the interim periods ended March&#160;31, 2026 and
March&#160;31, 2025. Although management believes that the disclosures in these unaudited financial statements are adequate to make the
information presented not misleading, certain information and footnote disclosures normally included in unaudited condensed consolidated
financial statements that have been prepared in accordance with U.S. GAAP have been omitted pursuant to the rules and regulations of the
SEC. These unaudited condensed consolidated financial statements should be read in conjunction with the Company&#8217;s financial statements
and notes related thereto and included in the Company&#8217;s Annual Report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025, filed with
the SEC on March 31, 2026. The&#160;interim results for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 are not necessarily indicative of the
results to be expected for the year ended December 31, 2026 or for any future interim periods.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000453" name="us-gaap:ConsolidationPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_847_eus-gaap--ConsolidationPolicyTextBlock_z16GlWrJgk23" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_862_zsaR3DxsBxgl">Basis of Consolidation</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The consolidated unaudited financial statements
include the accounts of AppTech, and wholly owned subsidiaries of which the Company is the primary beneficiary. All significant inter-company
accounts and transactions are eliminated in consolidation.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000455" name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates"><p id="xdx_84D_eus-gaap--UseOfEstimates_zT914fDoXf1a" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86C_zAdmr1qHy0Ed">Use of Estimates</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The preparation of the financial statements in
conformity with generally accepted accounting principles requires management to make estimates and assumptions that affect the reported
amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the
reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the reporting period.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000457" name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk"><p id="xdx_846_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskCreditRisk_zbYZdt9UPrTl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_869_zpXv0bIaNsw1">Concentration of Credit Risk</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Cash and cash equivalents are maintained at financial
institutions and, at times, balances may exceed federally insured limits of $250,000 per institution that pays Federal Deposit Insurance
Corporation (&#8220;FDIC&#8221;) insurance premiums. The Company has never experienced any losses related to these balances.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As of March&#160;31, 2026, <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskPercentage1_pip0_dp_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByBenchmarkAxis__us-gaap--AccountsReceivableMember__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByTypeAxis__us-gaap--CustomerConcentrationRiskMember__srt--MajorCustomersAxis__custom--TwoCustomersMember_zuXHxYwBKDl3" title="Concentration risk, percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskPercentage1" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_AccountsReceivableMember_us-gaap_CustomerConcentrationRiskMember_custom_TwoCustomersMember" id="Fact000459" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">36</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the accounts
receivable balance was generated from two customers. As of December&#160;31, 2025, one customer accounted for <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskPercentage1_pip0_dp_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByBenchmarkAxis__us-gaap--AccountsReceivableMember__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByTypeAxis__us-gaap--CustomerConcentrationRiskMember__srt--MajorCustomersAxis__custom--OneCustomerMember_z6CXArYobgj" title="Concentration risk, percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskPercentage1" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_AccountsReceivableMember_us-gaap_CustomerConcentrationRiskMember_custom_OneCustomerMember" id="Fact000461" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">16</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the accounts receivable

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">For the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026,
<span id="xdx_90A_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskPercentage1_pip0_dp_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByBenchmarkAxis__us-gaap--SalesRevenueNetMember__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByTypeAxis__us-gaap--CustomerConcentrationRiskMember__srt--MajorCustomersAxis__custom--TwoCustomersMember_zaOIecksRwSi"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskPercentage1" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_us-gaap_SalesRevenueNetMember_us-gaap_CustomerConcentrationRiskMember_custom_TwoCustomersMember" id="Fact000462" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">28</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the Company&#8217;s revenue was generated from two customers. represented <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--ConcentrationRiskPercentage1_pip0_dp_c20250101__20250331__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByBenchmarkAxis__us-gaap--SalesRevenueNetMember__us-gaap--ConcentrationRiskByTypeAxis__us-gaap--CustomerConcentrationRiskMember__srt--MajorCustomersAxis__custom--TwoCustomersMember_zAYYpxj6YUc"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConcentrationRiskPercentage1" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31_us-gaap_SalesRevenueNetMember_us-gaap_CustomerConcentrationRiskMember_custom_TwoCustomersMember" id="Fact000463" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">74</ix:nonFraction></span>% of total revenues.</p>

    <div id="xdx_236_zXEqar4MtqFk" style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 33%">&#160;</td><td style="width: 34%; text-align: center"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->10<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td><td style="width: 33%; text-align: right">&#160;</td></tr></table></div>
    <div id="xdx_23F_zksFBAITkS1f" style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>
    <!-- Field: /Page --></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_239_zM7hUfQX6mSj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000469" name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84E_eus-gaap--CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock_zI3D0YLefpV8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_865_zAjzg7pMgAbb">Cash and Cash Equivalents</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company's cash consists of cash on deposit
at its bank. Cash equivalents, if applicable, represents highly liquid investments with maturities of three months or less at the date
of purchase. Management determines the appropriate classification.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000471" name="us-gaap:CreditLossFinancialInstrumentPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84F_eus-gaap--CreditLossFinancialInstrumentPolicyTextBlock_zJ6rfox6Eqs4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86E_zx9qlEKea56d">Accounts Receivable and Allowance for
Credit Losses</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Accounts receivable are recorded at the invoiced
amount and presented net of an allowance for credit losses, when applicable. The Company estimates the allowance for credit losses using
relevant available information, from both internal and external sources, relating to past events, current conditions, and reasonable and
supportable forecasts. Historical loss experience is generally the starting point for estimating expected credit losses, which is then
adjusted for current conditions and reasonable and supportable forecasts of future economic conditions, as applicable. The Company regularly
monitors receivables for collectability and updates its estimate of expected credit losses at each reporting date.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Historically, the Company has not experienced
significant credit losses on its accounts receivable and, as a result, maintains an allowance for credit losses of $0 as of March 31,
2026 and December 31, 2025, respectively. The Company has not written off any material accounts receivable balances in the periods presented.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000473" name="us-gaap:PolicyLoansReceivablePolicy"><p id="xdx_84F_eus-gaap--PolicyLoansReceivablePolicy_z1H0luuLOoGf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_865_zsmevkNJp8e2">Loan Receivables and Interest Income</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Loan receivable represents amounts due to the
Company under contractual lending arrangements. Loans are recorded at their outstanding principal balance, net of any allowance for expected
credit losses, when applicable. The Company evaluates loan receivables for collectability on a regular basis and considers relevant factors
such as payment history, financial condition of the borrower, and current economic conditions.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Interest income is recognized over the term of
the loan using the effective interest method, based on the principal amount outstanding. Accrual of interest is discontinued when, in
management&#8217;s opinion, the collectability of principal or interest becomes uncertain. Payments received on nonaccrual loans are applied
to principal unless otherwise determined by management.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000475" name="us-gaap:DebtPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84A_eus-gaap--DebtPolicyTextBlock_zvb0flobA8uk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_867_zl1TTnRLLasl">Convertible Notes</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Convertible notes represent debt instruments that
may be converted into the Company&#8217;s equity securities upon the occurrence of certain events or at the option of the holder, in accordance
with the terms of the agreement. Convertible notes are recorded at their principal amount, net of any unamortized debt discount and issuance
costs.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company evaluates the terms of each convertible
note to determine whether any embedded features, including conversion options, should be accounted for separately or as a component of
the note. Debt discounts, including those arising from embedded derivative liabilities or other embedded elements, are amortized to interest
expense over the term of the notes using the effective interest method.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Upon conversion, the carrying value of the convertible
note, including any unamortized discount, is reclassified to equity.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23C_zK8qdg8V01nj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_231_zFbxBQlU4lh9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_234_zO2wpDQFsG0a" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 11 -->
    <div id="xdx_23B_zIBEUvHbZAo4" style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 33%">&#160;</td><td style="width: 34%; text-align: center"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->11<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td><td style="width: 33%; text-align: right">&#160;</td></tr></table></div>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23E_zau0zfPptvF9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000481" name="us-gaap:RevenueRecognitionPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84A_eus-gaap--RevenueRecognitionPolicyTextBlock_z5XlqkUuouEj" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86B_zualmumzohog">Revenue Recognition</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for revenue under Accounting
Standards Codification (&#8220;ASC&#8221;) 606 Revenue from Contracts with Customers, which provides a single comprehensive model for
entities to use in accounting for revenue arising from contracts with customers.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000483" name="apcx:SetUpFeesPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84C_ecustom--SetUpFeesPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zgB9w3Z6QQd8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86D_zgusUoPUOzB1">Setup Fees</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company provides one-time customer setup services
that include gathering and validating required compliance documentation for its banking partners and performing the technical integration
necessary to establish an operational merchant profile on the Company&#8217;s platform. As part of the setup, customers receive stand-alone
value by receiving a named bank account with our banking partner that they can use independent of us. Setup services are satisfied at
a point in time when the customer or subaccount is fully configured and enabled to transact on the platform. Revenue is recognized upon
completion of setup, which generally coincides with month-end billing.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000485" name="apcx:MonthlyPlatformAndTransactionBasedFeesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_846_ecustom--MonthlyPlatformAndTransactionBasedFeesPolicyTextBlock_zTnG0usDSipf" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_868_znpLPMJez2A4">Monthly Platform and Transaction-Based
Fees</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Monthly recurring platform access fees and transaction-based
fees represent consideration for continuous platform access and payment processing services and are recognized monthly as the services
are performed. Transaction-based fees, which represent variable consideration, are recognized in the period in which the underlying transactions
occur. Subaccount setup fees are recognized when the subaccount is established and made available for use.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><br/>
Customers are invoiced in arrears at month-end, and amounts billed but not yet collected are recorded as accounts receivable.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000487" name="apcx:MerchantProcessingServicesPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_849_ecustom--MerchantProcessingServicesPolicyTextBlock_zPKrhVB1ind9" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86B_z1FiCvNemVyj">Merchant Processing Services</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company provides merchant processing solutions
for credit card and ACH transactions. We act as an intermediary between merchants, who initiate transactions and banks that process them.
We collect either a flat fee, a fee for each transaction, and or a fee calculated as a percentage of its value, from both credit cards
and ACHs. Revenue is recognized when transactions are processed by banks or at month-end based on the processing activity. Payments to
channel partners are deducted from revenue.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000489" name="apcx:AccruedResidualsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84C_ecustom--AccruedResidualsPolicyTextBlock_zHwsqoYRyqh6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_866_zRpGnhO0A2sg">Accrued Residuals</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company pays commissions to independent agents
who refer merchant accounts. The amounts payable to these independent agents are based upon a percentage of the amounts processed by these
merchant accounts.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_238_zG1mcT7F8YWc" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_231_zcJfMhu9SAN1" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_233_zMjDOur40tf7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p></ix:exclude>

    <div id="xdx_233_zUKiic55VaMc" style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 33%">&#160;</td><td style="width: 34%; text-align: center"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->12<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td><td style="width: 33%; text-align: right">&#160;</td></tr></table></div>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23A_zgabYuNtuTP8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000495" name="us-gaap:GoodwillAndIntangibleAssetsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_845_eus-gaap--GoodwillAndIntangibleAssetsPolicyTextBlock_zXlJcYBGvoQ4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86F_zX9DkXkeewG8">Intangible Assets and Intellectual Property</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Intellectual Property</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company amortizes intellectual property based
on the estimated period over which the economic benefits of the intangible assets are expected to be consumed. Typically, the Company
amortizes its intellectual property, including patents and other identifiable intangible assets, on a straight-line basis. The amortization
periods generally range from three years to fifteen years, depending on the nature of the asset and its expected useful life.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Capitalized Software Development Cost</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company capitalizes certain costs related
to the development of its digital payment and banking platform, including employee compensation and consulting fees for third-party developers,
only when it is probable that the development will result in new or additional functionality. Costs incurred during the preliminary project
planning phase and post-implementation phase are expensed as incurred. The capitalized software development costs are amortized on a straight-line
basis over the estimated useful life of the asset.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Goodwill </span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for goodwill in accordance
with ASC 350, Intangibles &#8211; Goodwill and Other (&#8220;ASC 350&#8221;). ASC 350 requires that goodwill and other intangibles with
indefinite lives should be tested for impairment annually or on an interim basis if events or circumstances indicate that the fair value
of an asset has decreased below its carrying value. Goodwill represents the excess of the purchase price over the estimated fair values
of the net tangible and intangible assets of acquired entities. The Company performs a goodwill impairment test annually and more frequently
if an event or circumstance indicates that impairment may have occurred. Triggering events that may indicate a potential impairment include,
but are not limited to, significant adverse changes in customer demand or business climate and related competitive considerations. The
Company first performs a qualitative assessment to determine whether it is more likely than not that the fair value of a reporting unit
is less than its carrying amount. If it is determined that it is more likely than not that the fair value of a reporting unit is less
than its carrying amount, the Company performs a goodwill impairment test to calculate the fair value of the applicable reporting &#160;&#160;unit(s)
and compares it to its carrying amount. We recognize an impairment on any amount of the carrying value over the fair value. If the Company
determines that the implied fair value of a reporting unit is greater than its carrying amount, the goodwill impairment test is not required.
Management performed the analysis and no impairment was deemed necessary.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000497" name="us-gaap:ImpairmentOrDisposalOfLongLivedAssetsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_843_eus-gaap--ImpairmentOrDisposalOfLongLivedAssetsPolicyTextBlock_zUW2Bjic7nWk" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_865_zvZWHT9spcu5">Impairment of Long-Lived Assets</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Long-lived assets are reviewed for impairment
when there is evidence that events or changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount of an asset or asset group may not be
recoverable. Recoverability of assets to be held and used is measured by comparing the carrying amount of an asset or asset group to estimated
undiscounted future cash flows expected to be generated by the asset or asset group. If the carrying amount of an asset or asset group
exceeds its estimated future cash flows, an impairment charge is recognized for the amount by which the carrying amount of the asset or
asset group exceeds the estimated fair value of the asset or asset group. Long-lived assets to be disposed of by sale are reported at
the lower of their carrying amounts or their estimated fair values less costs to sell and are not depreciated. During the first quarter
ended March 31, 2026 and March 31, 2025, there was $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--AssetImpairmentCharges_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331_zPmFAXUBOB4g" title="Asset impairments"><span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--AssetImpairmentCharges_pn3n3_c20250101__20250331_zyVA3NpjHxM" title="Asset impairments"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000499" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetImpairmentCharges" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000501" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">0</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> in asset impairments.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_237_zx9cL3b7pvH5" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_235_zBzmAiSKoKD" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_231_zpzVl59EeGec" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i></i></b></p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000507" name="us-gaap:ResearchAndDevelopmentExpensePolicy"><p id="xdx_84A_eus-gaap--ResearchAndDevelopmentExpensePolicy_znmNWSPmKPq7" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86E_zz7lsNQtrsYl">Research and Development</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Research and Development (R&amp;D) expenses include
internal and outsourced service costs incurred to maintain the FinZeo and Infintus Pay, Inc platforms. Per ASC 730, R&amp;D costs are
expensed as incurred. Total R&amp;D expenses for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025 were approximately $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--ResearchAndDevelopmentExpense_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331_z10rVAWSqm3h"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ResearchAndDevelopmentExpense" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000508" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">375</ix:nonFraction></span>
and $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--ResearchAndDevelopmentExpense_pn3n3_c20250101__20250331_z5xSKDaRe6K"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ResearchAndDevelopmentExpense" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000509" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">780</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000511" name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_845_eus-gaap--EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock_zTHEzqcuQL6j" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_867_zPlRwPxI7az5">Per Share Information</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Basic net income (loss) per common share is computed
by dividing net income (loss) by the weighted average number of shares of common stock outstanding during the year. Diluted net income
(loss) per common share is computed by dividing net income (loss) by the weighted average number of shares of common stock outstanding
during the year, increased by the potentially dilutive common shares that were outstanding during the year. Dilutive securities include
stock options, warrants granted, and convertible preferred stock.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The number of common stock equivalents not included
in diluted income per share was <span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount_c20260101__20260331_zbSoUgcyvPg3" title="Antidilutive securities excluded from computation of earnings per share, amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount" contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" id="Fact000513" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">35,735,129</ix:nonFraction></span> and <span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount_c20250101__20250331_zuqbXPlL6K8b" title="Antidilutive securities excluded from computation of earnings per share, amount"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AntidilutiveSecuritiesExcludedFromComputationOfEarningsPerShareAmount" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-03-31" id="Fact000515" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">25,577,892</ix:nonFraction></span> for the three months ended March&#160;31, 2026 and 2025, respectively. The weighted
average number of common stock equivalents is not included in diluted income (loss) per share because the effects are anti-dilutive.</p>

  </table></ix:nonNumeric>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000531" name="us-gaap:CompensationRelatedCostsPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_847_eus-gaap--CompensationRelatedCostsPolicyTextBlock_zG7NwUpuOyCl" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86E_zV0mknkqjg65">Stock Based Compensation</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Stock options are valued using the estimated grant-date
fair value method of accounting in accordance with ASC Topic 718, Compensation &#8211; Stock Compensation. Fair value is determined based
on the Black-Scholes Model using inputs reflecting our estimates of expected volatility, term and future dividends. We recognize forfeitures
as they occur. The Company has several consulting agreements that have share-based payment awards based on performance. These agreements
typically require the Company to issue common stock to the consultants on a monthly basis. The Company records the fair market value of
the common stock issuable at each month end when the performance is complete based upon the closing market price of the Company&#8217;s
common stock.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000533" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingPolicyPolicyTextBlock_z1fyo3D71yl8" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_865_zCzIh2vYPNu7">Segment Reporting</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In accordance with ASC 280, Segment Reporting
(&#8220;ASC 280&#8221;), we identify our operating segments according to how our business activities are managed and evaluated. ASC 280
establishes standards for companies to report financial statement information about operating segments, products, services, geographic
areas, and major customers. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise for which separate financial information is
available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision makers (&#8220;CODMs&#8221;) in deciding how to
allocate resources and assess performance.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The CODMs have been identified as the Chief Executive
Officer and Chief Financial Officer, who reviews the operating results for the Company as a whole to make decisions about allocating resources
and assessing financial performance. Accordingly, management has determined that the Company only has <span id="xdx_90B_eus-gaap--NumberOfOperatingSegments_dxL_uInteger_c20260101__20260331_zrQUktnB9f88" title="Number of operating segment::XDX::1"><span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--NumberOfReportableSegments_dxL_uInteger_c20260101__20260331_zRLFa8wmA5I4" title="Number of reportable segment::XDX::1"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden: xdx2ixbrl0535"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden: xdx2ixbrl0537">one</span></span></span></span> operating and reportable segment.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The key measures of segment profit or loss reviewed
by our CODMs are revenue and operating expenses. Revenue is monitored by the CODMs to understand the performance of its verticals. Operating
expenses are reviewed and monitored by the CODMs to manage and forecast cash. The CODMs also reviews operating costs to manage, maintain
and enforce all contractual agreements to ensure costs are aligned with all agreements and internal budgets.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_234_z3a6XSrddBmc" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_230_z0H8umqg8e32" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p></ix:exclude>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_236_z9ra3nlFVs17" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000542" name="us-gaap:FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_849_eus-gaap--FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zDC7RR4mHbLb" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_86B_z41hGAytFAgd">Fair Value Measurements</span></span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company measures certain assets and liabilities
at fair value in accordance with applicable accounting guidance. Fair value is defined as the price that would be received to sell an
asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement date. The Company uses
valuation techniques that maximize the use of observable inputs and minimize the use of unobservable inputs.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company categorizes fair value measurements
within a three-level hierarchy based on the nature of the inputs used in the valuation, as follows:</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse">
  <tr style="vertical-align: top">
    <td style="width: 31px">&#160;</td>
    <td style="width: 24px"><span style="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 10pt">&#183;</span></td>
    <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Level 1</b> &#8212; Inputs are quoted prices in active markets for identical assets or liabilities that the Company has the ability to access at the measurement date.</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="vertical-align: top">
    <td>&#160;</td>
    <td><span style="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 10pt">&#183;</span></td>
    <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Level 2</b> &#8212; Inputs are observable for the asset or liability, either directly or indirectly, but do not include quoted prices for identical instruments in active markets. These inputs may include quoted prices for similar assets or liabilities in active markets, quoted prices in markets that are not active, and other observable inputs such as interest rates and yield curves.</span></td></tr>
  <tr style="vertical-align: top">
    <td>&#160;</td>
    <td><span style="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 10pt">&#183;</span></td>
    <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>Level 3</b> &#8212; Inputs are unobservable and reflect the Company&#8217;s own assumptions about the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability. These inputs are developed based on the best information available in the circumstances.</span></td></tr>
  </table>
<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s assessment of the significance
of a particular input to the fair value measurement requires judgment and may affect the classification of the asset or liability within
the fair value hierarchy.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company recorded a contingent consideration
of up to $1,000 thousand payable in cash upon achievement of specified performance targets. It was initially measured at fair value using
a Monte Carlo simulation and is recorded as a liability, subject to remeasurement at each reporting date with changes recognized in earnings.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">This contingent consideration liability is classified
as a Level 3 fair value measurement; it is remeasured at each reporting date with changes in fair value recognized in earnings. The following
table presents a rollforward of the liability for the period:</p>

    <div id="xdx_23A_zPcpN5qarO7j" style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="width: 33%">&#160;</td><td style="width: 34%; text-align: center"><!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->15<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td><td style="width: 33%; text-align: right">&#160;</td></tr></table></div>

<ix:exclude><p id="xdx_23D_zA0SSLWWXhOe" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000558" name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock"><p id="xdx_84E_eus-gaap--NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock_zopSVpakYC02" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline"><span id="xdx_867_z5ceB1LPhgBk">New Accounting Pronouncements</span></span></i></b>&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Financial Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;FASB&#8221;)
issues Accounting Standards Updates (&#8220;ASUs&#8221;) to amend the authoritative accounting guidance in the Accounting Standards Codification
(&#8220;ASC&#8221;).</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On November 4, 2024, the FASB issued ASU 2024-03,
<i>Income Statement &#8212; Reporting Comprehensive Income &#8212; Expense Disaggregation Disclosures</i>. This ASU provides guidance
to public companies regarding footnote disclosures of natural expense components (such as employee compensation, depreciation, and amortization)
included within each relevant income statement expense caption. The guidance is effective for public companies for fiscal years beginning
after December 15, 2026. The Company is currently assessing the impact of the new guidance on its financial statement disclosures.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In September 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-06,
Intangibles&#8212;Goodwill and Other&#8212;Internal-Use Software (Subtopic 350-40): Targeted Improvements to the Accounting for Internal-Use
Software (the &#8220;internal-use software update&#8221;). This ASU modernizes the accounting for internal-use software costs to better
reflect agile development methods. The Company is currently evaluating the impact of this guidance on its consolidated financial statements
and related disclosures. Per the guidance, the effective date of the ASU is for annual reporting periods after December 15, 2027.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In December 2025, the FASB issued ASU 2025-11,
Interim Reporting (Topic 270): Narrow-Scope Improvements. This ASU clarifies the applicability of interim reporting guidance and reorganizes
certain interim disclosure requirements within ASC 270 to improve consistency and usability. The guidance also introduces a disclosure
principle requiring entities to disclose material events and changes occurring after the most recent annual reporting period. The Company
is currently evaluating the impact of this guidance on its consolidated financial statements and related disclosures. Per the guidance,
the effective date of the ASU is for interim reporting periods within fiscal years beginning after December 15, 2027, with early adoption
permitted.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Except as noted above, the Company believes that
recent ASUs issued by the FASB either (i) provide supplemental guidance, (ii) are technical corrections, (iii) are not applicable to the
Company, or (iv) are not expected to have a material impact on the Company&#8217;s consolidated financial statements or related disclosures.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>&#160;</b></p>

</ix:nonNumeric></ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000560" name="us-gaap:OtherCurrentAssetsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--OtherCurrentAssetsTextBlock_z1wQvc1bCCie" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><b>NOTE 3 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82E_zh0hAHOV6qje">OTHER CURRENT ASSETS</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><b>&#160;</b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On April 11, 2025, the Company entered a three-party
agreement with one of its banking partners and software providers. Under the terms of the arrangement, AppTech agreed to pay a $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ContractualObligation_iI_pn3n3_c20250411__us-gaap--TransactionTypeAxis__custom--LicensingFeeMember__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--SoftwareProviderMember_z1Lzm4tGctd7" title="Contractual obligation"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractualObligation" contextRef="AsOf2025-04-11_custom_LicensingFeeMember_custom_SoftwareProviderMember" id="Fact000562" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">103</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand
licensing fee to the software provider. In addition, the banking partner held a $<span id="xdx_90E_eus-gaap--LongTermInvestmentsAndReceivablesNet_iI_pn3n3_c20250411__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--SoftwareProviderMember_zg5r6NXBj8ll" title="Loan receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LongTermInvestmentsAndReceivablesNet" contextRef="AsOf2025-04-11_custom_SoftwareProviderMember" id="Fact000564" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">250</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand note receivable bearing 8% interest with
the software provider, while the software provider held an offsetting liability to the banking partner. As part of this arrangement, AppTech
assumed the note receivable and offsetting note payable to the software provider and banking partner, respectively. AppTech further paid
the accumulated interest to the bank and established an interest receivable from the software provider. The note receivable matures on
June 2026, and may be converted early if a financing event occurs. As of March 31, 2026, the accrued interest on the note receivable was
$<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--InterestReceivable_iI_pn3n3_c20250411__srt--CounterpartyNameAxis__custom--SoftwareProviderMember_zcrh6oSy8ov" title="Interest receivable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestReceivable" contextRef="AsOf2025-04-11_custom_SoftwareProviderMember" id="Fact000566" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">51</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand.</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000572" name="us-gaap:IntangibleAssetsDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_800_eus-gaap--IntangibleAssetsDisclosureTextBlock_zT6lzABDJO41" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0"><b>NOTE 4 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82D_zTa8lDqtAXV6">INTANGIBLE ASSETS</span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Intellectual Property</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As part of the Infintitus Pay, Inc transaction,
the Company acquired intangible assets valued at $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets_iI_pn3n3_c20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfintitusPayIncMember_zKMAXugspS7i" title="Acquired intangible assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-31_custom_InfintitusPayIncMember" id="Fact000574" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">3,504</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand, which includes $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--Goodwill_iI_pn3n3_c20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfintitusPayIncMember_zr7GNsh8nec8" title="Goodwill"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Goodwill" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-31_custom_InfintitusPayIncMember" id="Fact000576" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,795</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand of goodwill. $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets_iI_pn3n3_c20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfintitusPayIncMember__us-gaap--IndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsByMajorClassAxis__us-gaap--TrademarksMember_zbnHreMbuT07" title="Acquired intangible assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-31_custom_InfintitusPayIncMember_us-gaap_TrademarksMember" id="Fact000578" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">322</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in trademarks
will not be depreciated, however, the remaining $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets_iI_pn3n3_c20251031__us-gaap--BusinessAcquisitionAxis__custom--InfintitusPayIncMember__us-gaap--IndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssetsByMajorClassAxis__custom--AcquiredTechnologyMember_zdwtfLC9NDV9" title="Acquired intangible assets"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessCombinationRecognizedIdentifiableAssetsAcquiredAndLiabilitiesAssumedIndefiniteLivedIntangibleAssets" contextRef="AsOf2025-10-31_custom_InfintitusPayIncMember_custom_AcquiredTechnologyMember" id="Fact000580" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,387</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand in acquired technology will be amortized on a straight-line basis over
60 months.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Goodwill</span></i></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On October 31, 2025, the Company completed the
acquisition of Infinitus Pay, Inc. The difference between the fair value of the purchase price and the net assets acquired is recorded
as goodwill. As of March&#160;31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the goodwill was approximately $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--Goodwill_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_zCcrja1k6Hn7" title="Goodwill"><span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--Goodwill_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_zdtjpsyb4bCb" title="Goodwill"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Goodwill" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000634" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Goodwill" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000636" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">2,956</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> thousand.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-01to2026-03-31" escape="true" id="Fact000663" name="us-gaap:DebtDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--DebtDisclosureTextBlock_zsu8mgDnPb59" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b>NOTE 6 &#8211; <span id="xdx_824_zs1LrbEFiRq2">NOTES PAYABLE </span></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="background-color: white">The Company
has a 30-year unsecured note payable with the U.S. Small Business Administration. The note payable incurred a $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentFaceAmount_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember_zEXPCTFMZSyc" title="Debt face value"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentFaceAmount" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember_custom_UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember" id="Fact000665" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">100</ix:nonFraction></span> fee upon issuance and
incurs interest at <span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_pip0_dp_c20260331__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember_zqH1lPxHDXy8" title="Debt stated interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember_custom_UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember" id="Fact000667" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">3.75</ix:nonFraction></span>% per annum. Payments totaling $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentAnnualPrincipalPayment_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember_zLF4un2yTDDk" title="Debt annual periodic payment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentAnnualPrincipalPayment" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember_custom_UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember" id="Fact000669" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">4</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand are due each year through the maturity date of <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentMaturityDate_dd_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--LongtermDebtTypeAxis__custom--USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember_zdAJGW2UROEe" title="Debt maturity date"><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_USSmallBusinessAdministrationMember_custom_UnsecuredNotePayable30YearMember" format="ixt:datemonthdayyearen" id="Fact000671" name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentMaturityDate">July 1, 2050</ix:nonNumeric></span>.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As of March 31, 2026 and December&#160;31,
2025, the balance of the note payable was $<span id="xdx_908_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--AllNotesPayableMember_zYA0hrlvg5A4" title="Note payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_AllNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000673" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">59</ix:nonFraction> </span>
thousand and $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--AllNotesPayableMember_zvtoPmmmFxEl" title="Note payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_AllNotesPayableMember" id="Fact000675" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">59</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand, respectively.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Convertible Notes</span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In fiscal year 2025, the Company entered into
multiple unsecured convertible note agreements with third-party lenders, with principal amounts ranging from approximately $300 thousand
to $360 thousand, bearing stated interest rates of 10%, and original issue discounts ranging from $50 thousand to $60 thousand. The notes
have terms of six to twelve months, with maturities between December 2025 and August 2026. As of March 31, 2026, one note was paid off
and two others remained outstanding.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Each note may be converted into shares of the
Company&#8217;s common stock at a fixed conversion price of $2.00 per share, subject to adjustment in the event of default, at which point
the conversion price may be reduced to the lower of $2.00 and 80% of the volume-weighted average price (VWAP) of the Company&#8217;s common
stock for a specified period preceding conversion. Certain notes include provisions for fixed monthly payments beginning after a defined
period, and others permit early conversion at the lender&#8217;s discretion.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, the
aggregate principal balance of these notes was approximately $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20260331_z4fkXk0ZUJjk" title="Convertible notes payable balance"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConvertibleNotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31" id="Fact000677" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">562</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand and $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--ConvertibleNotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20251231_zZKDtv8q8uPa"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ConvertibleNotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000678" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">783</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand, respectively, and accrued interest
totaled approximately $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--InterestPayableCurrentAndNoncurrent_iI_pn3n3_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--ConvertibleNotesMember_zOCP6v58V1b6" title="Accrued interest"><span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--InterestPayableCurrentAndNoncurrent_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--ConvertibleNotesMember_zmCSHgGYacZ" title="Accrued interest"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestPayableCurrentAndNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_ConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000680" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestPayableCurrentAndNoncurrent" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_ConvertibleNotesMember" id="Fact000682" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">31</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> thousand for both periods.</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i><span style="text-decoration: underline">Related Party Liabilities</span></i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><b><i>&#160;</i></b></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif">On
October 21, 2025, the Company entered into a revenue participation agreement with a related party for an initial $1,500 thousand
investment, which increased to $2,000 thousand in February 2026. The holder is entitled to 1.75% of gross contract revenue (subject
to increase up to 10%) and minimum monthly payments ranging from $4 thousand to $73 thousand through December 31, 2029, based on the
greater of the revenue share or contractual minimums. The obligation is secured by a pledge of 10% of intellectual property-related
revenues and 10% of the issued and outstanding equity of Infinitus Pay, Inc. The Company is required to repay the investment over
the final 18 months of the term. The agreement includes (i) a participant put option, exercisable beginning December 31, 2027,
requiring repayment at an amount that provides a 20% internal rate of return, and (ii) a Company call option permitting early
termination subject to payment of a premium designed to provide a 28% internal rate of return. As of March 31, 2026, the balance of
the participation liability was $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--ContractualObligation_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionAxis__custom--RevenueParticipationLiabilityMember_zAc8Eb2vnKed" title="Contractual obligation"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ContractualObligation" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_RevenueParticipationLiabilityMember" id="Fact000689" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">2,000</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand and interest paid during the three months ended March 31, 2026 was $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--InterestExpenseDebt_pn3n3_c20260101__20260331__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionAxis__custom--RevenueParticipationLiabilityMember_zlTbfXxbtXcc" title="Interest expense"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InterestExpenseDebt" contextRef="From2026-01-012026-03-31_custom_RevenueParticipationLiabilityMember" id="Fact000691" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">17</ix:nonFraction></span>
thousand.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">On October 29, 2025, we secured a $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_pn3n3_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--Oct2025NotePayableMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--InvestorAndBoardMemberMember_zQNz5JcE6vf4" title="Notes payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_Oct2025NotePayableMember_custom_InvestorAndBoardMemberMember" id="Fact000693" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="-3" scale="3" unitRef="USD">1,000</ix:nonFraction></span> thousand
loan with an interest rate of <span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage_iI_dp_c20260331__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--Oct2025NotePayableMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--InvestorAndBoardMemberMember_zRP654SmHrE6" title="Debt stated interest rate"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentInterestRateStatedPercentage" contextRef="AsOf2026-03-31_custom_Oct2025NotePayableMember_custom_InvestorAndBoardMemberMember" id="Fact000695" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">24</ix:nonFraction></span>% with a current investor and board member. The note is interest-only through March 2026, and thereafter
will be paid in full with ten equal payments of principal and interest ending on December 2026. The note is secured by our accounts receivable
and 10% of the Infinitus Pay, Inc stock.
